New `GET /admin/audit/facets`: counts per action, object type, outcome and person (top 20), each under every other filter but its own, plus the security count and the total. Filters apply at once and live in the URL (date presets 24 h / 7 / 30 days or a custom range; chips with Clear all). The list loads 100 at a time in a 640 px box, virtualised past 50 rows, "1–N of total". The detail sheet lists every detail field, links to the object, the model version in Model studio or the dashboard's versions, and filters to "Events from <person>" / "Events on this <object>". More object types link (data model, personal field, custom SQL).)*
- [x] Notifications centre page with read/unread and type filters. *(`/notifications` for everyone (board Notifications): All / Unread, type filter with per-type counts, Today and Earlier, mark one or all as read, Show more; the bell gains "See all notifications". `GET /notifications` adds `kind`, `cursor`, `limit` and per-kind counts. Decision (chosen): no notification preferences.)*
- [x] AI settings and usage (US-106) built here: settings form and ECharts usage charts (tokens, calls, cost, latency, blocked calls). *(`/admin/ai` (board AiSettings), Administer → AI settings and Platform in the sub-navigation; the overview's AI card links here. Form (RHF + Zod) on the existing `llm-settings` API: provider from the deployment allow-list, model per job with its fixed fallback, totals/counts and sample values toggles, monthly budget, cost rates per model, write-only key; saving a provider runs the gateway test call, a failure is shown and not saved. Usage for 7/14/30 days: calls, tokens, estimated cost and blocked tiles; calls, tokens, latency (median and p95) and blocked calls per day on the shared ECharts wrapper with table view; a table by model, task or person. Cost per day is in the tiles and table only.)*

#### US-147 · Motion and micro-interactions · S · FEAT-057 — `[x]` done (EXEC-018, branch `ux-foundation`)
As any user, I want the interface to respond smoothly, so that changes are easy to follow.
Depends: US-134.
- [x] Motion presets for dialogs, sheets, popovers, toasts and list insert/remove (≤ 200 ms); all off under `prefers-reduced-motion`. *(`design/motion.css`: `--tl-motion-fast/base/panel` 120/180/200 ms ease-out and one reduced-motion switch for every CSS animation and transition; `motion-*` utilities on Dialog, Sheet, Popover, DropdownMenu, Select, Tooltip; sonner toasts 400 → 180 ms; `MotionList` on the bell, the notifications centre and the audit filter chips. Motion runs as `LazyMotion` + `m.*` so the library stays out of the entry chunk. `src/test/motion.test.ts` checks durations and the reduced-motion rules.)*
- [x] Lucide icon set throughout; Lottie only where US-133 calls for it, lazy-loaded. *(The last text glyph (×) replaced. US-133 calls for no Lottie, so none is added.)*

#### US-148 · Accessibility, visual regression and performance gates · M · FEAT-057 — `[x]` done (EXEC-018, branch `ux-foundation`)
As QA, I want automated gates on the new UI, so that the redesign stays accessible, consistent and fast.
Refs: NFR-021, NFR-028; TEST-070. Feeds: US-127, US-128, US-130.
- [x] `@axe-core/playwright` on every redesigned screen in both themes: zero serious or critical issues. *(`e2e/a11y.spec.ts`, WCAG 2.2 AA tags, 28 screens × 2 themes on the live stack. Found and fixed: tabs-as-filters without panels (`aria-controls`), 16–20 px targets in the SQL field list.)*
- [x] Keyboard-only runs of J1, J5 and J9; contrast checked on all tokens in both themes. *(`e2e/keyboard.spec.ts`: Tab/Enter/Space/arrows/Escape and Ctrl+K only; J9 runs Creator request → Admin publish (fixing failing checks) → version history → unpublish. Found and fixed: focus not returned when a dialog or sheet opened from state closes (`ui/return-focus.ts`), a Tab trap in the people picker. `src/test/contrast.test.ts` checks every text pair ≥ 4.5:1 and edges, focus, axes and chart marks ≥ 3:1.)*
- [x] Playwright screenshot comparisons at 1280 px and 768 px for each screen; reviewed against the Claude Design screens. *(`e2e/visual.spec.ts`: 56 baselines in `e2e/__screenshots__` (macOS Chromium), live dev stack; times replaced and masked, growing lists clipped, Creator screens on a "Visual baseline" fixture the spec creates. Review found: Home's recent questions overflowing at 1280 px, request ids spilling out of error cards — fixed.)*
- [x] Bundle budget per route (Viewer route stays lean); no Largest Contentful Paint regression against the pre-redesign baseline on the dashboard viewer. *(`pnpm bundle:check` with `bundle-budget.json` (current + 10 %): entry 191 kB gzip, viewer 382 kB (438 at the start; the table view and drill sheet now load on first use). `e2e/perf.spec.ts`: LCP at 4× CPU, median of 7; pre-redesign build 176 ms (`e2e/perf-baseline.json`), new viewer ≈ 230 ms, gate = baseline + 80 ms. The viewer chunk now preloads during sign-in and the dashboard is fetched alongside `/me`.)*
- [x] Remove `styles.css` once every screen is migrated. *(Removed; the Dashboards and Shared with me lists moved to the DataTable with a New dashboard dialog; dead WidgetView, DrillPanel, DefinitionPopover and ErrorNote deleted; preflight is global.)*

## Phase P3 — Breadth and hardening

**Goal:** more sources, scale and release readiness. **Exit:** connector conformance suites pass on every engine; tenant isolation tests pass; NFR targets met; UAT signed off.

### EPIC-02 Connections and data model (part 2)

#### US-108 · Excel and CSV upload · L · FEAT-011 — `[x]` done (EXEC-024)
As an Admin, I want to upload spreadsheets as governed sources, so that teams analyse them with the same governance.
Refs: REQ-025 – REQ-027; API-011, API-068; DATA-013; ADR-012. Acceptance: **TEST-013, TEST-085**. Depends: DEC-07.
- [x] Multipart upload: `.xlsx`/`.csv` only, ≤ 50 MB, magic-byte check, reject macro-enabled/password-protected files (MSG-027), optional malware scan, checksum. *(`connections/files.py`; zip-bomb bound; UTF-8 CSV; scanner `TELA_FILE_SCAN_URL` fails closed.)*
- [x] Store at `tenant/{t}/files/{file_id}/v{n}`; sheet → entity; header row and type inference; flag merged cells, formula-only and mixed-type columns. *(Profiled by the runner; manifest kept per version.)*
- [x] Admin confirms sheets/headers/types and classification (personal-data columns flagged); retention required. *(Migration 0016; personal flags become model sensitivity.)*
- [x] Replace file → new version, re-introspection diff; widgets on removed sheets → Broken (E-13); model pins file version. *(Pending version + diff, activate/discard; `source_pin`; nightly sweep of expired, unpinned versions.)*

#### US-109 · DuckDB adapter · M · FEAT-011 — `[x]` done (EXEC-024)
As the runner, I want to query uploaded files read-only, so that spreadsheets use the same spec engine.
- [x] Download to tmpfs; in-memory DuckDB; views per sheet; `enable_external_access=false`, `lock_configuration=true`; interrupt at 20 s; cleanup. *(Deviation: converted once per file version into a DuckDB file in tmpfs (LRU) and opened read-only per execution, instead of re-reading the workbook per query; tables, not views.)*
- [x] `duckdb` dialect in `SqlCompiler`; contract test suite passes. *(Parity suite equals PostgreSQL; read_csv/COPY/ATTACH/INSTALL refused.)*

#### US-110 · Dataverse connector (TDS) · L · FEAT-012 — `[~]` built and tested against a fake environment (EXEC-024); live verification waits for DEC-09 / US-058
As an Admin, I want to read Dynamics 365 / TPG data directly, so that the PMO pack runs on live data.
Refs: REQ-028 – REQ-030; API-066; ADR-013. Acceptance: **TEST-014, TEST-067, TEST-086**. Depends: US-058 go decision.
- [~] Connection form: environment URL, tenant ID, client ID, secret/certificate, mode, tables; validation. *(Secret only; certificate credentials not yet.)*
- [x] MSAL client-credentials token injected via `SQL_COPT_SS_ACCESS_TOKEN`; ODBC Driver 18; 20 s timeout. *(Token packing unit-tested; the SQL Server stand-in cannot validate Entra tokens, so a dev-only SQL login replaces injection there.)*
- [x] Read-only verification of the application user's role privileges (or confirmed fallback per spike).
- [x] Introspection from `EntityDefinitions`: logical/display names, lookups → links, choice sets → labels.
- [~] Dataverse profile of the T-SQL compiler (unsupported constructs removed per spike). *(Provisional: no CTEs; confirm the subset in US-058.)*
- [x] PMO pack signature matches Dataverse logical names. *(Pack 1.2.0: statecode/statuscode labels; only documented logical names exist in the fake, so KPI-based measures stay Unavailable until GAP-009 is closed.)*

#### US-111 · Dataverse Web API fallback · M · FEAT-012 — `[~]` built and tested against a fake environment (EXEC-024); live verification waits for DEC-09 / US-058
As the runner, I want a Web API path, so that Dataverse works when TDS is disabled.
Refs: API-067. Acceptance: **TEST-087**.
- [x] OData v4 `$select/$filter/$apply` and FetchXML compiled from specs; GET only (no write code path).
- [x] Honour 429 `Retry-After`, max 2 retries, "Source is busy, retrying" (MSG-025); measures without a Web API shape → Unavailable.
- [x] Automatic offer of Web API mode when TDS is disabled. *(Test result `suggest_mode`; one-click switch on the Test step.)*

#### US-112 · MongoDB adapter and compiler · L · FEAT-013 — `[x]` done (EXEC-024)
As an Admin, I want MongoDB sources in the governed experience, so that document data is available.
Refs: REQ-031, REQ-032, REQ-081; API-065; ADR-014. Acceptance: **TEST-015, TEST-084**. Depends: US-059.
- [x] `connectionStatus` privilege check (only find/listCollections/listIndexes/collStats/dbStats); credentials never in the URI; TLS on. *(The built-in read role's full action list from MongoDB 7 rolesInfo is accepted: also changeStream, dbHash, killCursors, listSearchIndexes, planCacheRead.)*
- [x] Introspection by bounded in-memory sampling (default 200 documents); field paths, types, presence ratio; arrays as child entities; mixed types flagged.
- [x] `MongoCompiler`: scope `$match` first; approved `$lookup` with scoped nested pipeline; `$unwind` + regroup on root `_id`; `maxTimeMS`; `$limit cap+1`; stage allow-list incl. nested pipelines; groupings via `$switch`.
- [x] Custom SQL not offered (MSG-018).

#### US-113 · MySQL adapter · M · FEAT-005 — `[x]` done (EXEC-024)
As an Admin, I want MySQL sources, so that the TPG extract and other MySQL databases connect.
Refs: API-063. Acceptance: **TEST-006, TEST-078**.

- [x] `SET SESSION TRANSACTION READ ONLY`; `max_execution_time`; TLS on; `mysql` compiler dialect; contract suite on live container.

#### US-114 · SQL Server adapter · M · FEAT-005 — `[x]` done (EXEC-024)
As an Admin, I want SQL Server sources, so that the scope document's third engine is supported.
Refs: API-064. Acceptance: **TEST-078**.
- [x] pyodbc + ODBC Driver 18 (Encrypt on); not `sysadmin`/`db_owner`; `fn_my_permissions` check; `ApplicationIntent=ReadOnly`; `LOCK_TIMEOUT`; query timeout attribute. *(Also writer/DDL roles and object-level grants; no session read-only switch exists, so one SELECT only and every unit of work rolled back.)*
- [x] `tsql` compiler dialect (`TOP`); validator fuzzing specific to T-SQL (`WAITFOR`, `xp_cmdshell`, `OPENROWSET`, `OPENDATASOURCE`). *(The shared adversarial corpus runs per dialect; compiler ordering and grouping fixed for T-SQL and MySQL.)*

#### US-115 · Connection forms for remaining engines · M · FEAT-005/011/012/013 — `[x]` done (EXEC-024)
As an Admin, I want engine-specific forms with validation, so that each source is set up correctly.
- [x] MySQL, SQL Server, MongoDB, Excel/CSV and Dataverse forms per the connection form validation table. *(Plus sheet confirmation and file replacement with a diff.)*

#### US-116 · Multi-connection routing · M · FEAT-018 — `[x]` done (EXEC-024)
As a user, I want questions routed to the right source, so that I get answers when several connections exist.
Refs: REQ-033, REQ-034.

#### US-117 · Connector conformance matrix · M — `[~]` done for every engine (EXEC-024); the Dataverse nightly runs once the sandbox exists (DEC-09)

Acceptance: **TEST-077, TEST-078, TEST-067**.
- [x] Live containers for PostgreSQL, MySQL, SQL Server, MongoDB, DuckDB in CI; Dataverse nightly against the sandbox. *(CI job `conformance`; `scripts/conformance.sh`; `.github/workflows/dataverse-nightly.yml` skips until secrets are set.)*
- [x] Write attempts with the runtime identity are refused by every source. *(Straight to each source with the runtime login, guards bypassed; DuckDB read-only file; the Dataverse fake counts writes.)*

### EPIC-01 Identity and access (part 2)

#### US-118 · SCIM 2.0 provisioning · L · FEAT-004 — `[x]` done (EXEC-026)
As an Admin, I want users and groups synced from Entra, so that removing someone in Entra removes their access in Tela.
Refs: REQ-007, REQ-008; API-003; DATA-002, DATA-004, DATA-005. Acceptance: **TEST-005**.
- [x] `/scim/v2` ServiceProviderConfig, ResourceTypes, Schemas, Users, Groups; filters `userName eq`, `externalId eq`; pagination. *(`identity/scim.py`; Groups also take `displayName eq` and `excludedAttributes=members`, which Entra uses; other filters → 400 `invalidFilter`; SCIM error schema, `application/scim+json`. Proxy routes `/scim/v2/`.)*
- [x] Idempotent on `externalId`; `active:false` → SUSPENDED + sessions revoked; DELETE → DEPROVISIONED, records kept. *(Migration 0018. A retried create updates the same person; someone who signed in before is matched by the Entra object id of their sign-in, never by e-mail. Tokens issued before `users.sessions_revoked_at` get 401 `session_revoked`; a `revoke_sessions` task ends Keycloak sessions when `TELA_IDP_ADMIN_CLIENT_ID/SECRET` are set, otherwise it is skipped. Deprovisioned people keep their row and content and read as 404 over SCIM.)*
- [x] Membership changes recompute roles/scope and invalidate caches; per-tenant provisioning token; 600/min rate limit. *(`effective_roles_cache` recomputed and principals dropped per affected user; token created/rotated/turned off by the Admin, stored as a SHA-256 hash, shown once; limit in Redis per tenant (`TELA_SCIM_RATE_PER_MINUTE`). A deleted group loses its members, keeps its role mappings. Audit `scim.*`.)*

#### US-119 · Provisioning status and reconcile · S · FEAT-004 — `[x]` done (EXEC-026)
As an Admin, I want sync status and a Reconcile action, so that I can fix drift.
- [x] `scim_reconcile` hourly task; failures retried with backoff and alerted; ownership reassignment for deprovisioned users (E-17). *(Worker beat `scim-reconcile` at :40 → `POST /internal/scim/reconcile` (Celery retries 1 then 2 min). Reconcile retries due sign-outs (2^n min backoff, gives up after 5), removes memberships of removed people, adds missing revocations, refreshes role summaries, and reports people not provisioned from Entra, deleted groups still mapped and former users who own content; `scim.drift` / `scim.sync_failed` notifications when findings change. API: `GET /admin/provisioning`, `POST|DELETE /admin/provisioning/token`, `POST /admin/provisioning/reconcile`, `POST /admin/users/{id}/reassign-content`. Screen: Admin → Access → Provisioning.)*

### EPIC-11 Starter content (part 2)

#### US-120 · Starter pack updates · M · FEAT-047 — `[x]` done (EXEC-025)
As an Admin, I want to apply newer pack versions safely, so that starter content improves without touching users' copies.
Refs: REQ-113; API-048. Acceptance: **TEST-056**.
- [x] `pack_update_check` daily; notification with change summary. *(Worker beat `pack-update-check` 05:00 UTC → `POST /internal/packs/update-check`; one `pack.update_available` notification per newer version per model to everyone with `starter.manage` (`data_models.pack_update_notified_id`, migration 0017), e.g. "starter reports: 1 changed, 1 removed".)*
- [x] Measure changes go into a draft model version (approval required); update replaces starter reports only; affected published dashboards get drafts; copies untouched. *(API-048 `GET /admin/packs/updates`, `POST /admin/packs/{pack_id}/update` in `packs/updates.py`. Three-way merge onto the model draft (pack-changed items replaced, dropped removed, added added; the Admin's own items and edits kept); the version waits in `pending_pack_id` and approval switches to it. No model change → applied at once. Starter items carry their state, hidden roles and report over; changed reports rebuilt in place (dashboard drafts follow, live stays, owners notified `starter.updated`), dropped ones retired like RULE-017 (`starter.removed`). Screen: "Pack updates" cards on Admin → Starter content. TEST-056 in `test_pack_updates_api.py`.)*

- [ ] Container scan clean; penetration test of SSO, scope, custom SQL and export paths.

#### US-126 · Backup, restore and retention · M — `[ ]` (`retention_sweep` is scheduled but a stub until DEC-12)

- [ ] `retention_sweep`: turns 180 days, events/LLM logs 90 days, exports 24 h, uploads per policy, audit IP pseudonymised after 90 days, users anonymised 30 days after deprovisioning.

| Phase | S | M | L | XL | Stories |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| P0 | 2 | 13 | 1 | 0 | 16 |
| P1 | 2 | 26 | 15 | 0 | 43 |
| P2 | 16 | 83 | 23 | 1 | 123 |
| UX | 3 | 11 | 3 | 0 | 17 |
| P3 | 2 | 15 | 7 | 0 | 24 |

Plus 30 decision items (DEC-01 – DEC-30) and 6 prerequisites (PRE-01 – PRE-06) in Phase D.

Counts are a planning aid; re-size stories at refinement once DEC-01 – DEC-20 are settled.
